# 136: Chapter 136

A champion was no normal trainer. To assume so, was the height of folly. The difference between a champion and an average trainer, was as vast as the distance between Heaven and Earth.

In the first place, a single low ranking Gym Leader could handle dozens of average trainers at once, a top tier gym leader could defeat scores of them without breaking a sweat. And even the weakest of Elite Four could decimate a top tier Gym Leader.

It was in the name, they were the elites. But even the most elite of elites, fell short in comparison to a champion.

Champions could crush armies of hundreds of pokemon with a single one of their own. They were the ones that faced off against the incarnations of nature itself when legends ran amok, they were the ones who faced off against conceptual gods of space and time, they were the ones who carried the lives of entire regions on their shoulders.

To put it simply.

Champions were a big deal. And even a single one on one exhibition match against a trainer showing some potential in the gym circuit, could draw crowds upwards of fifty thousand and more with ease.

So when one of the most famous champions of all, one who was a champion of not one, but two regions accepted a battle from a young hot prodigy who blazed onto the scene with rare and powerful pokemon, it wasn't just Kanto or Johto who tuned in.

It was broadcasted across the entire world.

And people were glued to their seats with baited breath, eyes wide with shock as they watched the pokemon of a Champion, fall.

_________________________________________

"Yes!" Gary cheered, bouncing up atop the hood of his convertible where he'd been casually reclining with a laptop.

Fist pumped high in the air, his voice echoed through the wilderness around him, startling some pidgey above out of a tree. He was on his way to Porta Vista to meet up with his gramps when one of his girls casually playing with his laptop saw a trending live stream on Poketube.

Imagine his shock when it turned out to be the freaking champion of his region battling against Mr. Clap Their Cheeks himself.

He had no idea why the fuck Jord was battling the champion, it wasn't actually that long ago since he was talking to the brat over texts where he was bullshitting about wanting to meet his sister.

Well at least Gary hoped the kid was bullshitting, he didn't want a fuck boy like that anywhere near his older sister.

It was bad enough when Ashy-boy had a massive crush on her, he didn't need his new rival trying to bed her!

Besides, he had that hot booty shorts girl why did he need any more than that? He should be happy with just the one like any normal guy.

Gary conveniently ignored the squad of girls he toted around. Because much like his rival, the only hypocrite he liked was himself.

Either way, he was psyched and could contain his excitement at all. Jord had did it.

He'd brought down a champions pokemon. Albeit, his weakest pokemon by far, and Lance wasn't taking the kid seriously at all the kid wouldn't have landed a single one of his attacks that actually mattered if Lance had.

But, he'd still managed it.

His greatest rival, had defeated one of Lance's pokemon, was actually capable of hurting it. Which meant, since Jord was around Gary's level, he could do it to.

And it confirmed for him, he was on the right path!

"I should send that asshole something as thanks." Gary smirked to himself, fist still raised in the air.

Definitely not his sisters number though.

Fuck no.

__________________________________________

Ear ringing shouts of jubilation and whooping cheers thundered in Misty's ears and she winced in annoyance.

Jord was battling Lance.

Somehow, Misty couldn't find it in herself to be surprised. From the very moment she met him, he seemed to have a plan in mind for rising through the ranks at a rapid speed.

Even if his common sense was lacking, but then he was still younger than most trainers starting out by a couple years so fair enough.

Then again, this was the guy that caught just the other day wrestled a Gyarados and Dragonite into submission and whom when she last talked to, was lounging across a throne of evolutionary stones worth millions like a throne.

Jord and common sense didn't seem to get along well.

And this battle just showed that even more.

Jord had beat one of Lance's pokemon. That was the crazy thing. Misty wished she could actually pay more attention to the battle going on or put her thoughts into order.

But she wasn't given the chance. Because Ash and Brock were crowded around the screen in their room at the Pokemon Centre, alongside Pikachu, Scizor and Riolu.

Not to mention that annoying prankster of a Gengar that she knew was hiding in Ash's shadow right now.

She could only catch glimpses of the battle going on because of them crowding around it.

But it wasn't just that.

Her phone was constantly buzzing with messages or ringing with calls. From her sisters. Especially Violet.

The damn bimbo's had been bothering her off and on for a while about Jord, it only got worse when he started trending with his Dragonite and Gyarados, not to mention his Blastoise.

And they hadn't stopped messaging her ever since this battle with Lance went live and took the region by storm.

'Maybe if you didn't snub him for using a damn Raticate you'd be able to call him yourself.' Misty snorted. Stupid Violet, always the most superficial of her three older sisters and that was saying a lot.

Or not.

Misty smirked as she remembered that Jord had intentionally snubbed her sister right back when she came on to him after he beat Lily in his Gym Battle, because he didn't want to ruin his chances with her.

Either way, it was annoying though. Jord had gotten pretty good 'stock' with her sisters ever since his gym battle at Cerulean City. It had risen sharply when he trended on Poketube. And now it had risen massively again, probably even more so after he beat Lance's Dragonite.

Especially the way he casually bragged about giving a girl a Dragonite Lance claimed was easily worth twenty five million.

Gold diggers across the region probably had their eyes turn into pokedollars signs.

An uncomfortable sensation lingered in her stomach at the thought. But it wasn't because of them. At least not entirely.

The movie girl was definitely ahead of her right now with Jord. She sighed in annoyance.

She wasn't sure how to do about that-

So she put it out of mind for the moment to focus on what was going on right now, and on that point.

"Will you idiots move out of the way and let me see as well!" Misty growled in annoyance from behind the group of idiots blocking her view.

She got why Pikachu and Scizor were out, they both lost to Jord's Fearow, which just brought down Lance's Dragonite as well. But why was Riolu out? Why…just why?

It was so annoying!

__________________________________________

The student council room of Naranja Academy was alit with the noise of a cheering girl.

She was the only one there, but her cheering more than made up for it. Atop her desk, sat a laptop.

And the feed of a live stream directly from Kanto played out on the screen.

And!

Nemona!

Was!

So!

Excited!

"Ooooh, I wish he was from here!" the girl bit into her fist, vibrating with anticipation at the thought. Things worked a bit differently in Paldea than most regions.

If he was a trainer over here, he'd have had to start out from Naranja just like every other trainer. Which meant she could have met him.

And battled him!

That confidence! That boldness! The endurance! The sly strategizing! The way he knew his pokemon and trusted them to give it their all even in the face of someone like Lance!

"Gah, he's so cool!" she squealed, reaching up to cup her cheeks and vigorously shake her head, her green bang and ponytail bouncing jubilantly with the motion.

From her estimates, she was sure she was stronger right now than the boy and could defeat him in a battle. But, from everything she heard so far he'd only been a trainer for two months.

This kind of growth in only two months and the way he and his pokemon rose to the occasion to fight Lance? Even if she beat him, he'd just rise up to challenge her again after improving rapidly and maybe beat her!

And then she could use that as a chance to grow and re-challenge him and beat him.

And then he'd do the same and beat her, and then she'd to the same and then he'd to the same and then she'd do the same!

He would be the perfect rival!

Nemona wanted him, she wanted him so bad! How could she get him though? 'Maybe I could invite him here?' she wondered, head tilting to the side in thought.

Not to brag, but she had pretty big pull in Paldea. She'd already gotten all eight gym badges, her family was high up in authority, and she was the student council president of Naranja Academy!

Maybe she could do something with that?

__________________________________

"Now that's what I'm talking about!" Rosa whooped, pumping a fist into the air as she jumped up and down, her voluptuous curves bouncing and jiggling along with her, almost coming straight out of the scanty lade underwear that she was wearing…and nothing else, "This is what I expect from my future shota boytoy husband!"

It said a lot about her state of mind, that Hilda's shock was too great to even tell off her cousin.

Such was the craziness of the situation. Jord…had beaten Lance's Dragonite. Sure, he'd used four pokemon to do it and it didn't seem like Lance was taking him all that seriously still going by how lax his commands had been.

But he'd still brought down Lance's Dragonite.

Again, his weakest one, but that didn't make it weak.

It was a champions pokemon after all.

She should be excited, and glad and proud. And Hilda was, but overshadowing them, a sense of worry formed in the pit of her stomach.

Jord was strong, there was no doubt. And he could definitely take care of himself. But…he was still so early into his journey. Still so young.

All this limelight so quickly…it drew so much attention. Would he really be alright? If this was just tow months into his journey, and at how young he was now…

Hilda shuddered to imagine what kind of crazy escapades he'd get into going forward from here.

And that wasn't even into taking into account the normal things that came with the infamy he'd gained now. People all over would have designs for him to play into, or make them soon. And what about reporters and such? With his bold confidence bordering on absolute arrogance, and the way he just did and said whatever he wanted, they could easily twist things he said or situations he was in around to paint him in a bad light.

Everyone loved a hero.

Everyone loved a rising star.

And everyone loved a rebel.

She knew that as an ironclad fact from all her experience growing up in the show bizz.

But more than all three of them combined, she also knew that the people loved to see such a person be dragged down as well. Because people were not perfect, they were prone to envy, they were prone to anger at those that made them look bad.

And Jord made a lot of people look bad with his knowledge and skill. He wasn't even the normal age for a starting trainer, yet he made some trainers decades his senior look pitiful.

And all those who would envy him and smother their feelings of inferiority with anger, would love to see him dragged down into the mud with them. Because it would make them feel better about themselves.

Damn Team Plasma, if only they didn't steal Rosa's pokemon, she'd still be back in Kanto with Jord right now and she'd be able to shield him from some of that, and coach him on how to deal with the ones who'd try to consistently twist his narrative.

"Hey cuz!" Rosa's shout broke Hilda from her thoughts and she looked to see her cousin cupping her large breasts, bouncing them up and down…weighing them? "What kinda underwear does he like, do you know? Like does he have a colour preference or style? Like thongs, g-strings, maybe crotchless?"

And of course the gold diggers.

Not that she'd ever call her cousin a gold digger. Because she wasn't really interested in money, and was more pokemon mad. It just so happened that rare and powerful pokemon were either very hard to find, or very expensive.

"He prefers girls called Hilda, so shut the hell up and put some clothes on you damn exhibitionist." Hilda retorted with a roll of her eyes.

"Alright, guess I'm changing my name then, you can be Rosa now," Rosa shrugged without missing a beat, "Also, no. Just cuz you're jelly I'm hotter than you isn't any reason for me to cover this sexy body of mine, especially since I know you're totally calling Jord in a little while for sure. I'll let my shota husband see the goods in advance, get him all riled up and ready for me!"

"Will you stop calling him that, you're from Unova, not Kanto you twit," Hilda huffed, "And you are in no way, shape or form, hotter than me." she added, with a pointed glare.

"Your mother." Rosa replied maturely, sticking her tongue out.

"That doesn't even make sense!" Hilda's eyebrow literally twitched.

"Your face doesn't make sense." Rosa shot back, pulling her tongue back into her mouth and giving her a shit eating grin.

"That's rich coming from some chick who hit every branch on the way down the ugly tree after you were born!" Hilda narrowed her eyes, "You're so ugly we thought auntie had an affair with a Garbodor!"

"Yeah, well your ass is so fat when you walk down the street people think you're a wild Snorlax!"

"Your chest is so saggy people think you're a guy carrying around a Stunfisk!"

# 137: Chapter 137

The utter shock of his Dragonite falling, was almost palpable in the air. Jaws opening and closing, failing to find words, eyes so wide they looked like they were almost popping out of their sockets.

And more.

Good.

Lance resisted the urge to smirk even as he recalled his Dragonite while his young opponent whooped and hollered happily as he brought back that incredibly impressive Fearow of his.

It was a victory well earned.

Albeit, one that would have been impossible for the young boy to claim as he and his pokemon were right now, if he and Dragonite had taken them seriously from the start.

It was not a thought to cover any embarrassment from losing a pokemon to a child so young, or anything overly prideful like that, but simply fact.

That did not make it any less impressive in Lance's own eyes. He had a good feel for young Jord, going by the rough trainer profile they had documented of him personality wise and his actions up to this point, and from what he'd gleamed from the boy in the small time they'd spent together up to this point today.

There was a fire to the boy, alongside a cockiness and self assured gait that reminded Lance a bit of himself when he was just starting out, at a similar age as Jord too. Though, while he'd definitely took advantage early on of the popularity he'd garnered when he was younger and foolisher, especially with the opposite sex, he'd definitely not had quite the…grandiose way of going about it Jord did.

Albeit, while he had started out on his journey at thirteen, he'd been training under the masters of the Blackthorn dragon clan, the Wataru since he was but a toddler.

Jord did not have that same backing he did, yet Lance could admit wholeheartedly, that at the same age, he would have been crushed in a battle.

He wasn't quite sure how the boy had gotten to this point, with all the rare and powerful pokemon he'd gotten his hands on, except that he was sure the boy didn't just stumble upon them.

'Perhaps he's a powerful clairvoyant?' Lance mused idly. Clairvoyance was one of the psychic powers humans tended to have the least talent for, and very few came to mind who could drudge up anything worthwhile with the ability, like Olympia the gym leader of Anistair City in the Kalos Region.

It seemed unlikely, but was not impossible.

Honestly, Lance did not really care how the young boy had managed to get to this point. Others might have been suspicious, fearing a threat or just plain envious of the boys success and wanting to share in the pie or drag him down.

Lance was just pure excited to see the kind of heights Jord would reach in the future. And, he wanted to nurture that potential. Which was why he and Dragonite did not go all out to begin with and gave the boy and his pokemon a chance to show off and spread his name.

'Not to mention, with all those rare pokemon, he'll be a target.' Lance mused. That was, unless all those deplorable fools out there saw what he was capable of and thought his strength was too much of a risk to take on for the massive reward.

And he did not disappoint. The physical ability of his pokemon alone was through the roof, though their power did not quite match their physical abilities, but that would come with time and age. Beyond that though, was the sheer courage Jord's pokemon had and the trust they had in him, obeying his orders without hesitation even in the face of his Dragonite that could crush buildings with a swing of his arm.

Give the boy and his pokemon a few years, and they would be true monsters of battle. Lance almost shivered in excitement at the thought.

"Are you ready Jord?" Lance called over as he traded Dragonite's pokeball for another on his belt, a grin on his face that he simply could not contain, "My next pokemon is much stronger than Dragonite." he warned.

There was some that may have admonished him for outright declaring one of his pokemon stronger than the other. But all pokemon were different, where some would have felt admonished themselves for having such a thing said, or become depressed, dragon types were in general a whole other ball game.

Power and strength was the very birth right of pokemon of the dragon type and those with dragon traits. It was their very instinct to seek power and strength, and there was no better instigator for them to grow to new heights than being compared to another and being found lacking.

This battle alone, would for sure push Dragonite to all new heights of strength from seeing that it was not just those above him he would have to worry about, but fear those below like Jord's own Dragonite and Milotic and Fearow chasing after him and possibly surpassing him.

"If I wasn't would it change anything?" Jord called back, raising an eyebrow at him, switching Fearow's pokeball with one of the other eleven on his belt.

Lance merely continued grinning and did not say a word in reply.

"Didn't think so." the boy snorted, enlarging the pokeball in his hand.

Lance did the same, and then both of them unleashed their next pokemon at the exact same time.

A towering Charizard burst onto the field in front of Jord, spreading a pair of massive wings and unleashing a powerful roar that generated enough wind to ruffle Lance's hair from over fifty feet away.

Impressive.

A trio of snarling aggressive roars formed into a cacophony in front of Lance himself though in reply as his Hydreigon appeared in a burst of bright to face off against Charizard.

Quite a few in the watching crowd released cries of shock and fear at his choice of pokemon. And Lance couldn't blame them, Hydreigon's savage eyes filled with a snarling lust for violence and battle, and his trio of roaring, snarling mouths could be quite terrifying.

"Hydreigon, huh?" Jord audibly clicked his tongue, arms crossing across his chest. There was a wariness in his eyes, but no fear.

And his Charizard, did not back down at all, just like him. It stared down his snarling Hydreigon, with an aggressive growl on its lips and fire in its eyes.

Good, good.

It wouldn't do for him and his pokemon to be intimidated by a mere Hydreigon. Granted, his Hydreigon was no…mere Hydreigon and the difference in power between this Hydreigon and the Dragonite he led with-

Was larger than the difference between Jord's own Dragonite and his.

# 138: Chapter 138

"Hydreigon, Dragon Pulse!" Lance decided to start things off with this round. He wanted to push and see more of Jord and his pokemons abilities when backed into a corner.

A savage howl was released from Hydreigon's trio of heads, multicoloured light exploding into existence in his trio of maws, before unleashing a three piece set of gargantuan multi-coloured beams of pure destruction that formed into a trio of titanic roaring dragon maws of pure power.

One on the right, one on the left.

And one straight down the middle.

And with their sheer size and speed, it cut off any avenue for Charizard to escape between them.

They hit nothing.

When the trio of monolithic beams of power passed by and harmless flew off into the sky in the distance with nothing to show for it, it was to reveal a large hole in the ground.

'Dig, huh?' Lance grinned.

A classic.

Though impressive it could find the time and room to dig such a hole in the face of Hydreigon's attacks.

And going by what he'd seen of Jord so far-

"LR, protect, M, hydro hump." Lance ordered.

The right and left heads of Hydreigon roared, both conjuring a double layered green spherical barrier around his body.

Just in time for the ground beneath Hydreigon to buckle and burst open and a familiar orange scaled winged beast to shoot forth, both arms clad in claws of pure draconic energy.

And a familiar aura of power suffusing around Charizard's body.

'He used Dragon Dance to enhance Charizard's speed and strength to dig faster.' Lance nodded approvingly as the twin Dragon Claw attacked smashed into Hydreigon's twin protect.

A sound like a gong being struck rung out, and the first barrier rippled noticeably under the blow, but that was all.

And Hydreigon was already prepared to counter.

The twin barriers dropped and Hydreigon's main head unleashed a massive spiralling gout of pressurized water point blank at the Charizard.

Only to miss…as Charizard went limp and rolled downwards back into the hole it had previously made.

"Swords Dance, then again!" Jord shouted.

A moment later, Charizard burst back out of the hole, the green aura of Dragon Dance around its body, being infused with a crackling red piercing through it almost like electricity, arms infused once more with twin Dragon Claw attacks.

Enhancing the attack even further huh?

"Surf." Lance commanded simply. The tactic to dodge back into the dig hole was good for sure, but what would he do in the face of an attack so wide and encompassing such a thing wasn't viable?

What would he do in the face of an attack like that, three times over?

A trio of glittering blue spheres of hydro energy formed in Hydreigon's three mouths, before erupting and bursting forth.

A trio of titanic waves, one alone enough to create a fifty feet high and fifty feet wide tsunami, formed into one monstrous wave of all encompassing crushing water.

"BLA-"

He heard Jord begin to shout, before it was drowned out by the roaring monstrous wave, blotting him and Charizard both from sight.

Lance felt the ground shake ominously beneath him as if an earthquake had begun and sweat began to form on his brow as a sudden, roaring heat made itself known.

Bright orange light flashed, shimmering up through the wave before a massive explosion erupted and blew the entirety of the massive wave of water away.

Lance blinked as the entirety of the massively cratered battlefield came back into view.

Steam sizzled from the battlefield into the air, and he found the Charizard down on one knee, one hand clenched into a fist and buried deep in the battlefield itself.

"Oh-ho?" Lance's lips curved into a grin, "Blast Burn huh, that's really impressive."

Not only was it impressive that Jord's Charizard was already capable of using the penultimate fire type attack, but that quick thinking to use it to blow away the convergent trio of surf attacks and actually have the power capable of doing so.

it was all just impressive, very very impressive.

Though, looking at the Charizard, the aura of dragon dance and swords dance now gone, and noticeably exhausted, it did not come without a price.

Despite its impressive size, that Charizard was still so very young, just like its trainer.

He'd like to see what more it was capable of, but now wasn't the time to give them the chance to recover, it was the time to push them further and back them even further into a corner and see how they replied.

"Charge Beam, go." Lance ordered.

Hydreigon wasted not a single moment, trio of maws opening and three crackling bright yellow spheres forming in them.

Before unleashing them in a trio of massive yellow electrical beams that converged into one titanic beam of pure electrical power.

"Ground it!" Jord shouted over the roaring beam of yellow power.

Lance watched in interest as the exhausted Charizard forced itself up, claws gleaming silver alongside its tail and thrust both arms out to catch the electrical beam head on, at the same time it thrust its tail down into the ground itself.

And he still watched as the beam of power slammed into Charizard, and did absolutely nothing, it barely even winced as the attack washed over it.

'That's second time he's done that this battle.' Lance blinked. Specifically to electric attacks too.

He wondered how that worked?

He put it out of mind though as he noticed an electrical current form of Hydreigon's body. Perfect, he'd gotten the power boost that Charge Beam could provide.

Twice by his reckoning going by how thick the voltage was.

Charge Beam was perfect for Hydreigon. Each usage had a rather large chance for temporarily enhancing the users power. And Hydreigon could fire three at once, giving three chances at once for the boost.

Now then, what would Jord and Charizard do now? Four times now he and his Charizard had managed to overcome attacks that should he ended the battle right then and there.

What would they do now in the face of Hydreigon with his power boosted twice over from Charge Beam?

"Left, shock wave. Right, Surf. Main, Draco Meteor." Lance grinned as he upped the ante.

# 139: Chapter 139

An attack that would follow Charizard no matter how fast or much it dodged, a massive wide ranged attack, cutting off any avenue forward and a rain of meteors from above.

Even if he tried to Dig again, the Surf attack would just be drained into the hole and follow it.

"Rush it! Quick Attack, Dragon Dance!" Jord hurriedly ordered in reply.

To his credit, he was very quick on the ball. He already knew there was no chance of dodging here, and Charizard obviously didn't have a move to defend here like Protect, though even if it did, it would have been futile.

With a beat of its huge wings and thrusting itself forward with its tail buried in the ground, the Charizard burst forward at blurring speeds, an explosion of white power erupting from its body at the same time a familiar green aura suffused its body.

It was a very, very impressive speed.

It was just too bad the level difference between his Hydreigon and Charizard, was just so vast.

Before Charizard even cleared half the distance towards Hydreigon, it unleashed its attacks.

A massive blue bolt of power erupted from the left head, while a familiar sphere of hydro power erupted outwards from the right forming into a wave almost double the size and width just one of his previous Surf attacks were.

While a massive orange spere of burning draconic power shot up and out of Hydreigon's middle head and ascended high into the air, swelling in size until it was fifty feet wide, before bursting apart and raining down in a rain of glowing orange meteors, all swerving down towards Charizard.

One of the meteors slammed into Charizard's right wing and exploded. Charizard roared in pain and slammed into the ground, the momentum it had built up forcing it into a roll, dragging its scales roughly across the ground.

It wasn't even down for a blink of an eye before it was up with a roar, but by then it was too late.

The Shock Wave burst forward at blinding speeds and was in its face before it could-

Lance's eyes widened as Charizard's mouth opened and a massive gout of heat exploded outwards meeting the Shock Wave attack head on.

They converged together as they met, swelled up, and exploded.

Charizard was blasted back like a rag doll, bouncing across the battle field like a stone being skipped across the surface of a lake.

'Amazing!' Lance's eyes shone with glee. Such determination! He could feel the blood beginning to pound in his ears, feel his heart beat begin to thunder in his breast.

Barring that Aerodactyl he'd just caught, how did Jord install such determination, such loyalty, such raw refusal to give up in his pokemon?

With the distance Charizard had gained from being thrown back, it avoided the Draco Meteor that had been aimed specifically for where Charizard was rushing.

All that was left, was the Surf attack rushing forward.

But…seeing Charizard weakly trying to push itself up, it was enough.

Or so Lance thought.

"Let's go, Charizard!" Jord's shout was not a counter, or anything close. It was simple encouragement.

A gleam entered Charizard's eyes at the voice of its trainer, and a roaring snarl erupted from its own maw. Lance blinked as a shimmering orange aura of power shimmered into existence around its body and it pushed itself to its feet once more.

"Blaze…huh?" Lance laughed lightly.

"Flamethrower!" Jord punched a fist out towards Lance himself in sheer defiance.

That was it! Yes, this was the kind of person he liked best! To Jord and his pokemon it didn't matter what opponent was in front of them, how overwhelming powerful they were or how big the mountain they had to ascend and overcome was.

They would charge it head on without hesitation!

'Just like us!' Lance laughed inwardly as Charizard's head reared back, red hot flames forming in its maw, before thrusting its head forward and unleashing a massive compressed torrent of flames that could have given one of Hydreigon's dragon pulse beams a run for its money!

The sheer force and heat behind it tore straight through the Surf attack, outright obliterating it and roaring forth towards Hydreigon!

If that was how it was, then Lance wasn't going to back down either. if Jord and Charizard were coming at them full force head on with all their power right here and now, then he and Hydreigon would reciprocate in kind!

"Hyper Beam!" Lance punched his own fist out, mirroring his young opponent.

Hydreigon's trio of heads snarled as one, three pitch black crackling orbs of energy forming in their own maws, before being unleashing a moment later in a trio of massive pitch black beams of pure destructive power.

That then converged together into one titanic, monumental colossal beam of ruination!

Blaze enhanced flamethrower smashed into the convergent Hyper Beam and the sheer force behind their collision, created a shock wave so powerful Lance himself felt himself get pushed back a few steps and even heard screams of shock and fear from those watching even over the roar of the attacks colliding, a circular crater forming under the attacks.

Despite the size difference between them, the blaze enhanced flamethrower held steady in the collision for an impressive two full seconds before the combined Hyper Beam pushed it back and began to steadily overpower it.

Bit by bit, split moment by split moment, the massive gout of flame was pushed back until it was all but dwarfed by the pitch black beam of destruction.

Yet, Charizard did not give in, did not falter and fall.

"Full power Charizard, give it everything you've got!" Lance blinked as he heard Jord's voice roar over the colliding attacks.

He couldn't even see the boy over the massive attacks clashing against each other, he could barely even make out the Charizard.

Charizard's wings flared out mightily, the orange blazing aura around its body roiling like a thrashing wave as it increased with a burning intensity-

And then the white hot flamethrower expanded massively with all the intensity of a supernova, with all the raging brilliance of a dying rebellious star that refused to go quietly into the night.

The massive black beam of ruin was suddenly pushed back massively, Charizard's roar of defiance echoing through the air.

Lance grinned, "Let's give it our all as well then Hydreigon!" the master of the dragon type and champion of two regions declared, "Nasty Plot!"

A crackling aura of pure pitch black power howled into existence around Hydreigon at his order, his already enhanced power rising even further beyond.

And with a roar, the trio of heads using their Hyper Beam, unleashed their enhanced power.

The pitch black beam of power swelled to sizes beyond scope, increasing easily twice over.

And pushed back with monstrous power against the massively blaze enhanced flamethrower, tearing through it with all the power of a raging force of nature, destruction incarnate itself.

The flamethrower was overwhelmed within moments, pushed to the very brink until all that was lift was struggling embers that refused to die-

Before they too were consumed by the beam of pitch black destruction and it washed over Charizard who thrust both claws out to meet it head on as one last feeble resistance.

And then the beam itself having met its target, erupted into a monstrous explosion that dyed the world black for an instant, blotting out the sun itself.

There was silence across the battlefield as it dyed away, only broken by the sound of reigning debris falling from the sky and slamming into the ground.

And when the smoke cleared, it was to show Charizard-

Still standing?!

Lance's eyes widened in utter shock and a howl of excited glee erupted from Hydreigon's three mouths.

Beaten and bloodied, scales marred with wounds from head to toe, the Charizard glared towards Hydreigon with bright defiant slitted green eyes.

And took one step forward, right arm coming out, claws gleaming with shimmering green draconic power-

Before Charizard's eyes promptly rolled into the back of its head and it fell face forward towards the ground.

Yet, its face never made contact with the ground as a familiar red light swept over Charizard's body and it disappeared.

Revealing Jord, pokeball in his hand, having returned the pokemon before it could fall.

And a smile on his face despite the fact that from start to finish, Charizard had been completely overwhelmed.

__________________________________________

"Oh, you're approaching me?" Hydreigon smirked.

"I can't beat the shit out of you if I don't get close." Charizard cracked his knuckles.

# 140: Chapter 140

That Hydreigon, is a fucking monster.

I stared down at the pokeball in my palm, containing the pokemon who'd just faced off against Lance's Hydreigon and been completely overpowered. In the grand scheme of things, Charizard had only been able to put up a token resistance and never even landed a single attack.

Yet I couldn't have been prouder. Charizard might not have been able to do anything to Hydreigon in the end, but my boy smashed through attack after attack of that monstrous beasts, and even held his own briefly in a full of clash of attacks and completely exceeded my expectations.

Nobody in their right minds, could call Charizard weak. I hope that scrub loser Cross was watching and see's the amazing pokemon he gave up because he is simply too much of a loser ass scrub beta soy boy bitch to have drawn out even a fraction of my beastly Charizard's potential.

"Amazing work mate." I praised the pokemon within the pokeball quietly, before placing his pokemon on my belt, and grabbing the very first pokeball atop said belt after to replace it.

Time to go for broke.

How do we beat this monster? I have no earthly fucking idea. Pidgeot has no good attacks for this thing, beyond maybe turning its own dragon type attacks against it with Mirror Move.

That isn't even getting into the craziest thing about this Hydreigon. Each of its three separate heads, is capable of using attacks. Stacking three of the same attack at once for one gargantuan overpowered version, or using different attacks.

Fucking hell I wouldn't be surprised if one head can use Rest to heal up while the other two stay awake, and at the same time have them buff with moves like Dragon Dance or Nasty Plot, while the last protects it with barrier attacks like Protect and such.

I wonder, is that kind of thing possible for other pokemon with multiple heads? Like Dodrio…or Exeggutor perhaps?

Exeggutor might not have the kind of move pool to draw from a Hydreigon has, but it still isn't too shabby. I could have one throw up a Sunny Day while having Chlorophyll as an ability to enhance its speed, one head to teleport and protect, another to heal and buff with sun enhanced synthesis and shit and then have the last firing off instant solarbeams and more.

'Thoughts for later.' I shook my head, turning my attention to the battlefield, my eyes glancing at the absolutely huge Hydreigon that was easily twice the size of what was listed in the pokedex I'd grown up with, then over to Lance who was waiting for me, a massive grin on his face.

He was actually enjoying this battle, huh?

Well, far be it from me to let him down then, right? Besides, this battle isn't at all about winning. Don't get me wrong, the fact we managed to take down his weakest Dragonite is incredible, beyond words.

But in the end, this is all about showing my and my pokemons worth here and now.

And who better to do that, than the biggest try hard of them all?

"Let's go, Pidgeot!" I shouted, pressing the button in the middle of the pokeball held within my palm and releasing my starter pokemon from within.

In a bright flash of light, Pidgeot burst onto the field and spread his massive wings out wide and proud, "GEEEOOOT!" he barked loudly straight at the Hydreigon in challenge.

In response, the dark almost glowing trio of eyes belonging to the Hydreigon lit up with an almost palpable excitement and the huge three headed dragon unleashed a trio of deafening, heart shaking roars right back.

Six dark draconian eyes glared into sharp avian ones and a pressure settled over the battlefield, and made my heart thunder in my chest.

"Pidgeot, huh?" Lance in contrast barely seemed to pay it any attention, "And what a big one it is. This is your starter pokemon isn't it?"

I swallowed, pushing down and smothering the emotions Pidgeot and Hydreigon's glare off invoked in me, "That he is," I forced a smirk onto my face, "And my strongest by far."

Albeit, Pidgeot has one of the least expansive move sets amongst my pokemon. I should really work on that, huh?

"Then this should be a really good battle then!" Lance laughed, "All of your pokemon so far have gone above and beyond my expectations, so I'm expecting quite the match from your undisputed strongest!"

"Then we won't disappoint," I laughed back, which reminds me, "Pidgeot, Mirror Move!" I quickly ordered, taking advantage before he could use another attack with Hydreigon.

After all, the last attack he called for, was not Hyper Beam.

"Geot!" Pidgeot barked, flapping his wings and nodding, his eyes lighting up with a bright silver power.

And then an aura of darkness, the exact same as the one currently coating Hydreigon's body flared into existence around Pidgeot as he copied Nasty Plot and boosted his special attack power.

Of course, Pidgeot didn't actually know any attacks right now that the boost would be good for. But again, Mirror Move is key for this purpose.

"Oh, sneaky," Lance laughed, "Let's try this then shall we? Shock Wave, separate."

Hydreigon's trio of maws opened wide, crackling blue electricity buzzing into existence in each mouth before firing off in a trio of crackling massive bolts of sizzling power towards Pidgeot.

Was he testing to see if Pidgeot could cancel out electric attacks like my other pokemon he'd seen do it so far?

Alright then.

"Pidgeot, quick attack straight up!" I quickly ordered, and in a split moment, my try hard birb exploded upwards with a powerful beat of his wings, soaring high into the sky, a trail of burning white power trailing from his body, "Now rush it with Wing Attack!" I added as the trio of huge electric blue bolts swiftly changed direction and chased Pidgeot into the air.

Time to pull a Goku.

And Lance, you are the Frieza for this little roleplay game of mine.

Pidgeot shot down like a speeding bullet, so fast I could only make out a bit of a blur as he swerved down avoiding the shock wave attacks and rocketed towards Hydreigon with glowing white wings, the trio of electrical bolts quickly turning to chase him once more.

"Dragon Pulse." Lance simply countered the second Pidgeot was within point blank range.

"Spin right!" I shouted in a hurry, already prepared. After all, I didn't think for a second that Lance would be taken off guard just by something leading Shock Wave back to his pokemon.

Wing Attack was not for attacking, it was strengthening Pidgeot's wing strength for a brief moment.

Pidgeot beat both off his wings against the air with all his might and threw himself to the side, using all the momentum he'd built up with his quick attack enhanced flight speed.

At the same time he did that, all three of Hydreigon's mouths opened up, orbs of rainbow light energy forming into swirling spheres of power, before erupting into a trio of multi coloured beams that conjoined into one massive one, forming into the shape of a huge leviathanic serpentine dragon.

And missed Pidgeot completely.

But not the trio of crackling massive blue bolts of electricity that met it head on. they smashed into each other, swelling together, before erupting in a massive explosion of electrical and draconic power that washed over the Hydreigon at near point blank range.

Pidgeot unfortunately didn't escape the massive collision of attacks unscathed, the size of the explosion sweeping over his body briefly before he burst out of the side, his feathers scorched slightly.

But my number one try hard bird wouldn't go down to just that much after all.

And now is the perfect chance to take advantage of that copied Nasty Plot buff.

"Mirror Move!" I punched my fist out with a whoop!

"GEOT!" Pidgeot flared his wings out stopping himself mid air and opened his beak wide open, a familiar sphere of multi coloured energy swirling into existence within it, before he thrust his head forward, handsome stylish plumage swaying powerfully in the gale force winds being generated by the huge explosion caused by Hydreigon's attacks, and unleashed the attack in the form of a large serpentine dragon of pure rainbow coloured energy straight towards Hydreigon's previous position.

The roaring dragon of energy soared forth into the explosion and a moment later the area thundered with another explosion as the Dragon Pulse attack hit its target and detonated with world shaking force!

And then we waited with baited breath for the massive smoke from the explosion kicked up by the multiple hugely powerful attacks to dissipate.

Just before it made contact though, the middle head of the Hydreigon growled, eyes flashing green and then a green spherical barrier swarmed into existence around its body, just in time for the Dragon Pulse to smash into it, and wash over it like a wave against a cliff side.

"Rest and Roost," Lance ordered, crossing his arms and the grin on his face having only widened as the left head of Hydreigon dipped down further and fell asleep, while the right rose up with a snarl, a familiar energy colour washing over its body, the same I'd seen Pidgeot, Fearow and such use multiple times, "That was quite the tactic, not many would think to try and use shock wave against the user instead of blocking it or attacking it to destroy it, if they even knew it followed in the first place and didn't just try to dodge, but you even predicted that leading it back to Hydreigon alone wouldn't be enough and baited us into attacking, huh? Your battle instincts and strategy are incredible."

Shit. I should have expected Lance would be more Cooler than Frieza. He wasn't a shithead dumbass that relied on his natural abilities and heritage to get where he is alone after all.

And beyond that, Hydreigon could stack fucking healing moves? Are you fucking kidding me? That's bullshit!

"Quick Attack into Wing Attack!" I ignored him and quickly ordered, "Smash that barrier open!"

With the injuries Hydreigon had, and expending all that crazy amount of energy it had at this point, no way it could keep it up for long right? If we just hammer it down quick enough, we'll win this!

"Pidgeot!" Pidgeot barked out a battle cry and shot forward so fast with a beat of his wings I heard the sound barrier shatter around him, and the earth itself rumbled as he was upon protect barrier an instant later smashing both wing attack enhanced wings into it with all his power.

A sound like a gong being struck with a gigantic hammer rung out and thundered in my ears and the barrier rippled noticeably from the impact.

And then Pidgeot's wings moved as a blur as he hammered the barrier over and over again at bullet like speeds with a barrage of wing attacks.

'I really need to teach some pokemon feint for this protect bullshit!' I growled inwardly, fists clenching.

I know this isn't about winning, but this is bullshit, we had this! Or we would have if our opponent wasn't a bullshit more or less shonen protagonist pokemon champion!

I should have fucking remembered that from the beginning. Every single champion may as well have been a shonen protagonist already having completed their initial story.

Which meant, I should never even give them a chance to to recuperate, because somehow, someway, they'd pull bullshit like this out of their asses!

This is a good reminder for next time I battle Ash or something though!

Pidgeot's blows sounded like a machine gun turret going off from the rapid fire blows against the protect barrier-

And within just a few seconds, a sound like shattering glass rang out as the protect barrier broke under Pidgeot's rapid fire wing attacks!

"NOW!" I roared.

"GEOT!" Pidgeot roared back in compliance, both wings swinging down at Hydreigon like a pair of glowing guillotine blades.

Only, just as he was about to make contact, both the smaller heads of Hydreigon rose up and my eyes widened as a familiar pair of crackling yellow orbs of electricity came into view.

The Rest head was awake already!?

Twin beams of yellow electricity erupted and slammed into Pidgeot before he could make contact and my massive try hard bird shrieked in pain as the beams smashed him high into the air all the while electrocuting him, all the while he struggled against them with all his might.

Because my try hard bird wouldn't go down so easily!

"Spite!" Lance quickly ordered, and the main heads eyes glowed with crimson red energy that shot out in a pair of twin laser like beams and washed over Pidgeot as well.

And I watched as Pidgeot…went limp?

What….?

I watched bleakly as the twin Charge Beam attacks dissipated after a few moments and Pidgeot dropped from over fifty feet in the air to crash into the ground, hard.

What the fuck was that? Spite was supposed to get rid of an attacks move PP, so what the fuck did it do-

My eyes widened, "Spite drains stamina?!" I gaped in realisation as Pidgeot shakily began pushing himself up on both wings, panting in pure exhaustion, not pain.

I know the difference when it comes to Pidgeot after all. I'm well aware of his exhausted expressions at this point.

"First time running into it I see," Lance mused with a nod, "Well, you're still so very young and only just really started, it's obvious you won't have come across everything yet. But yes, you're right on the money Jord, Spite is a handy little technique that massively drains the stamina of the target."

"But wait," I shook my head, "That doesn't explain how one of the heads of your Hydreigon woke up so quickly!"

"Oh, that? That's just a little trick of mine," Lance laughed, "Hydreigon knows the attack Uproar. You probably didn't notice, but when Hydreigon's right head dipped back down and looked like it was resting, it actually was using a low powered version of Uproar to wake the other head up."

…And Uproar had a few effects when used, one of them being waking up sleeping targets hit by it. And since Hydreigon had multiple heads and could have one of them resting at a time…

He could basically recycle using Rest and take care of any of the risks that came with using it.

Fuck.

On top of that, between the Roost and Rest combo, Hydreigon was looking pretty much in tip top shape again.

"Now, how about to finish this up, we show something cool?" Lance's grin widened to almost manic proportions, "Lemme ask you Jord, do you know how the attack Payback works?"

I knew how the game version worked.

"It converts damage taken by the user into an attack." I replied evenly, clicking my tongue. I had feeling it wasn't going to be that simple though.

"Mostly right," Lance laughed, "In fact, it converts damage taken over a period of time into power. Even damage that has already been healed. And a fun fact as well, it is amplified by the boost Hydreigon gets from the Charge Beam attacks and Nasty Plot."

So basically…fucking Revenge Counter then?

Hydreigon the Dragon Sin of Wrath up in this bitch, huh?

Pokemon are fucking bullshit man.

I nodded, "Alright, bring it on then, let's see this bag bad attack of yours Mr. Champion, because Pidgeot isn't done yet!"

At my words, Pidgeot shook his head and spread his wings wide once more, "GEOT!" be barked in challenge towards Hydreigon once more.

"Yeah, that's what I've come to expect from you both Jord!" Lance laughed once more, happily, excitedly, "Let's go Hydreigon finish this off with-

"Mirror Move!" I quickly cut him off before he could finish.

Just before he finished and just before Hydreigon took his orders, I threw out mine.

And Pidgeot's eyes glowed with bright silver power.

And then an ominous crimson glow replaced it.

Twin beams of crimson energy erupted from his piercing avian gaze and slammed into Hydreigon while it and Lance were distracted.

A surprised grumbling roar left all three of Hydreigon's heads as Pidgeot began draining its energy with the copied spite.

And a second later, the floating Hydreigon dropped to the ground from exhaustion as its energy was drained by Pidgeot.

'You shouldn't have confirmed spite did that, and you should have just finished us off!' I roared inwardly, a smirk splitting my face.

Because Lance, you might have healed all the damage Hydreigon took. But it didn't recover its energy. And between the battle against Charizard and now Pidgeot, that Hydreigon had fired off a shit load of ultra powerful attacks!

Multiplied by three!

The true weakness of all shonen protagonists, was their need to monologue!

"Keep it up and rush it with Wing Attack!" I quickly ordered. The attack would exhaust Pidgeot to keep up for sure-

Because Mirror Move had one weakness I had discovered over using it since Pidgeot had learned it. It amounted to using two attacks at once.

And with his energy already massively drained, Pidgeot won't be able to keep it up for long!

But we don't need long!

"Geot!" Pidgeot barked and spiked his massive wings through the air, throwing himself into a dash through the air, all the while glaring crimson red beams into Hydreigon's body, draining it, keeping it pinned.

Pidgeot was on it in moments, wings glowing with the power of Wing Attack and slamming them into Hydreigons main head and smashing it into the ground with concrete shattering force.

It barely hit the ground before one of Pidgeot's talon'd feed kicked it in the stomach launching it back up, before Pidgeot headbutted it straight in the main face once more, glaring crimson red beams straight into it-

Before chopping it in the neck with one of his glowing wings, and then thrusting out one of his feet in a rising thrust kick that stabbed his talons into Hydreigon's chest, gouging them into its scales.

"Close Combat!?" Lance shouted in shock.

No, but it did look like that, didn't it?

Pidgeot was a blur of demolishing force, talons clawing into Hydreigon, head smashing into it with bone breaking force, wings glowing with power and crushing its body with each, beak shooting out with steel piercing power.

"I FUCKING TOLD YA! DAT BIRD IS A FRIGGIN MONSTER!" I heard someone shout loudly, they sounded kind of familiar actually, but I couldn't pin them while I was focused on Pidgeot giving it his all.

The blur of attacks lasted for just over five seconds of constant motion, before the red glare erupting from Pidgeot's eyes sputtered out and he lost momentum, tumbling back on his feet, though not before driving Hydreigon into the ground once more with one massive glowing white wing before the power behind it too fizzled out.

Pidgeot's legs trembled and almost gave out, he in fact began falling back, before he thrust both massive wings out, beating against the air itself and forced himself back to his feet.

Pidgeot panted in exhaustion, eyes fluttering with weakness, on the very verge of falling unconscious.

It was taking everything he had left just to keep standing.

Hydreigons main head rose up from where its body lay limp and hissed out a growl of defiance at Pidgeot, before falling limp.

Unable to continue.

"…You're a sly one, huh Jord?" Lance mused aloud, "That was quite the reversal, I have to admit." he said, as he casually returned Hydreigon to its pokeball.

"I'm a great a many things," I agreed, unashamedly, "And the loser of this battle sadly. Pidgeot can still go on of course, but I'm gonna throw in the towel here and now, I know where we stand more or less now." I said, as I lifted Pidgeot's pokeball and returned him to it, letting him rest.

Pidgeot actually has nothing left. But I have to protect his pride as well as his trainer after all. He wasn't backing down from another fight here, I was.

And that was nowhere close to a champion. Me and my pokemon have brought two of Lance's pokemon down, but only because he never once took us seriously and let us strut our stuff.

But, we're on the right path.

And I just showed the entire world that we were.

_________________________________________

Decided to upload the full fight in one chapter instead of three separate as is my usual for these kinds lengths.

All my stories are way farther ahead on my Pa---tr---eon if you guys wanna read ahead, the link to it is in any of my story summaries.

# 141: Chapter 141

For a few moments, as the battle drew to its ultimate conclusion, there was silence all around us.

Whether it was shock, awe, or just plain old confusion I couldn't be sure entirely, nor did I really care. My attention actually was more on the gleaming pokeball in my palm.

A proud smile on my face.

"Way to go mate." I praised the try hard bird within. We hadn't won, not even close, and Lance had obviously been taking it easy on us. We are nowhere near the level of a champion.

But let me put it like this. If an average Caterpie knew Electroweb and fired it off at Pidgeot, it would hardly do anything to him, even with him being weak to electric attacks.

It was simply because of how much more powerful Pidgeot was, he was on a completely different level. Though not something as arbitrary as simply numerical levels.

Why think this? Because, while Pidgeot and my other pokemon weren't even close to the level of Lance's, they were at a level where they could actually hurt them.

A level that simply could not be ignored even by the strongest.

And that in of itself was an achievement as far as I'm concerned. After all, one only had to look at Paul's battle with Cynthia to understand. Even as someone on Ash's level at Sinnoh, and with quite the strategy prepared, Paul fell short simply because Cynthia's Garchomp was so far above his pokemon in level that it didn't even matter that Garchomp was locked in place and took a full on Frenzy Plant from Paul's starter pokemon to the face.

It did fuck all.

The fact Pidgeot and my other pokemon simply couldn't be brushed off like that, told me we were on the right path, or a right path at least.

And that was amazing to me.

Clapping drew me from my thoughts and I looked up to see Lance boldly walking across the decimated battlefield towards me, his cape mantle swishing majestically from his shoulders as he did, clapping loudly and proudly, a big grin on his face, "That was a splendid battle Jord!" his voice boomed out from his lips, "Give it a few years and you're going to be a real monster." he praised me as he came to a stop in front of me and stopped clapping to hold one hand out to me.

I took it, shaking his hand, "Gimme six months." I grinned back.

Lance barked out a laugh, "Well considering your progress so far in only two, another three times that….well, you might be right!"

Before I could say anything else, the crowd of reporters erupted into a frenzy of cheers and questions.

"Champion Lance, how is it a trainer so early into his career has managed to take down two of your own esteemed pokemon!?"

"Jord, how do you train your pokemon for them to be so powerful!?"

"Champion Lance, is this perhaps you deciding to take on an apprentice for the first time?"

"Jord, there was a big focus on your love life during this battle, care to comment? What about this mysterious girl you are involved with caught your attention?"

Lance rolled his eyes at the last comment as we finished shaking hands, but I personally felt a smirk spread across my face.

Lance's eyebrows rose as I turned away from him and looked at the reporter who had asked that last question, it was that guy from Kanto News, "The first thing that caught my attention about her, can't really be said during this kind of interview, never mind live I don't think." I shrugged.

After all, the first thing that I noticed about Hilda had been that big ol' badonkadonk of hers.

"Yes, it's best not to go into detail on that part." Lance snorted in amusement, crossing his arms

The reporter from Kanto News blinked owlishly at me for a moment, before his eyes widened in understanding, "Ah…I see," he coughed, "Okay…then how about defining features that can be commented on, perhaps her personality? Surely this mystery girl must be quite astounding to catch the eye of one such as you, a rising star!"

"I'm a rising star?" I asked with a blink, looking to Lance.

"Well, you will be now, with your big debut here your name is gonna be on everybody's lips for at least the next few days, and you're gonna have a ton of eyes on you with what you've accomplished and how well you did in our battle," Lance shrugged, "Usually something like this would be more gradual, but you've kinda just exploded onto the scene."

"Huh, neat," I nodded in understand before turning my attention back to the reporter from Kanto News, "So other things about her I liked then? Well I mean beyond the obvious, she has really pretty eyes, a bright vibrant blue and her hair is like fluffy chocolate. And then there was the banter, she's great at that."

Let me see how much I can embarrass Hilda.

…Granted, it probably won't be all that much considering she's a former actress and would be used to stuff like this.

From there I went into a long tangent of bullshit about things I liked about Hilda, and some more that just popped into my head for shits and giggles.

"I see….could it be that you have already found the one you want to settle down with already?" the reporter asked, his cheeks flushed.

I think he's feeling a little embarrassed now to have had me go into this crap. Poor guy, this is why shame is useless.

"Well I'm still single for the moment, but that's for me to know mate, and you-" I looked him in the eyes and grinned at him, "To dot dot dot."

"I see." was all the man said in response.

I think he was flustered.

# 142: Chapter 142

"Jord, is there anything else you'd like to say aloud at this moment? Your plans going forward, what your aims are? Anything?" another reporter jumped in, this one with a cameraman right behind her.

I was about to say no, when a thought appeared in my head and a wide smirk spread across my face.

"Well, there is one last thing I'd like to say," I nodded and pointed straight at the camera, "Hey Cross, you're watching aren't you? How'd you like my Charizard? Feeling like an idiot now after you tossed him away for thinking he was weak? Just goes to show how amazing he actually is when trained by somebody who isn't a complete scrub like you."

And with that said, I closed down any other attempts at questioning me by turning on my heel and making my way towards the pokemon centre.

My job here is done.

I showed how amazing me and my pokemon are, I hopefully embarrassed Hilda and I insulted Cross on live television being broadcasted to the entire world.

Truly my genius is astounding.

___________________________________________

"Well, that's one way to end an interview," Lance commented as he followed me inside the pokemon center and I was actually astounded by my how empty it was, "Who is this Cross exactly?"

"Just a scrub," I shrugged and turned to him now that we were in the much quiet lobby of the pokemon center, "I actually ran into him when I got into Cerulean City and just before I met Hilda, he was a jackass that thought he was hot shit and tossed away his Charmander after I beat him in a battle using my Beedrill. Course I then caught the Charmander who is now my Charizard."

Lance clicked his tongue, "Sadly those types are while not common, not exactly rare either, fools that only see a pokemon for their current strength and ability and none of the patience required to properly raise them," he shook his head, "Though, blasting him on live television like that probably wasn't the most mature choice."

"Can't be helped, I'm a young brat after all, not even as old as most trainers are when starting out," I beamed brightly and innocently at him, "I simply don't know what I'm doing, I'm not mature yet after all."

"And I completely believe you," Lance nodded, snorting. He totally didn't, "Well it's not like you gave out his ID or anything, just his first name so there shouldn't be any harm. Moving on though, now that our battle has finished, let's finalize the deal over purchasing Aerodactyl's from you."

Mmm, yes.

Through the time we'd spent battling, I'd had plenty of time to finalize my thoughts on Lance and his clans offers and on my demands, "Beyond the first at a hundred million, I'm restricting your clan from breeding them for ten years," I stated, "So any others you purchase will be at forty million."

"Hmm, ten years huh?" Lance mused, stroking his chin and nodding, "A period of time that will be well enough for you to go down in the history books as the first owner of an Aerodactyl and the one that bred the species back into existence."

"That and I'll be widely known as the only person people can get the pokemon from over that period of time," I pointed out, "That's a lot of prestige to my name."

"True, though there will be those angry at you for it, for limiting the amount that can be bred and making it harder for others to get an Aerodactyl." Lance pointed out.

"Haters gonna hate, and most of them will just be common basement dwellers anyway," I snorted, "There's always gonna be people that hate you for being better than them and putting in effort to rise up the ladder and better yourself when they can't be bothered to do it themselves. Just entitled sad little shits."

"Well, you're not wrong I suppose, just odd you already understand that at your age, but then nothing about your achievements at your age make sense, so not worth thinking on," Lance shrugged, "Either way, those terms are acceptable, now we just need to find what pokemon can actually breed with Aerodactyl."

"No need to worry," I stole the famous line of an amazing bluenette, and best girl before I met Hilda, "I already know a bunch of pokemon Aerodactyl is compatible with. Honestly, it might actually have been fate that you turned up here to give me the offer, because you're one of the best bets for Aerodactyl's kids to be born even stronger."

And presented me with an opportunity.

"Oh?" Lance raised an eyebrow with interest, "I won't ask how you know, but the way you said that is telling enough. So Aerodactyl are compatible with some dragons then?"

"Yup," I bared my teeth happily as I crossed my arms and nodded my head, "Which leads me to my last demand.

Something you have a better chance of providing than anyone else. I want a strong Noivern or Altaria, preferably one that has had some training from you."

"A strong female Altaria or Noivern then, huh?" Lance mused, "I understand your thought process. Not only would you be getting a strong pokemon out of this, but it would ensure the baby Aerodactyl are born even stronger."

He cupped his chin for a moment, before nodding, "Fair enough, this works to both our benefits as well, it speeds up the time needed to find a compatible female and the starting point of the infants," Lance said aloud, "I've got a few of both species that aren't a part of my main team that I've been training up on the side, which would you prefer?"

Huh, as expected of the dragon master himself.

….Damn what one should I go for then? Altaria had a mega form and would turn fairy then and would be a good counter for other dragons and fighting types and such. But Noivern are amazing themselves as well, those sonar abilities could come in really handy.

But, I have to admit I was always more partial to Noivern. But if you get an Altaria it totally has to be female right? Aesthetics are important!

_____________________________________________

Unknown to Champion Lance, there was one more reason Jord had wanted a strong female Altaria or Noivern from him to breed with Aerodactyl.

"I can't be around to keep his punk ass in line all the time, so I'll get him a dommy mommy to do it for me, bwahahahahahahaha!" Jord laughed at his evil master plan.

# 143: Chapter 143

…As it is though, Noivern are pure dragon and flying types, which I already have covered with Dragonite. Altaria sheds the flying type when they mega evolve.

And yeah call me sexist, but if I have an Altaria, it needs to be female. Same way a Gardevoir does or a Sylveon.

"Let's go with Altaria." I decided with a shrug after a few moments of thought. Either way, it was going to be a powerful dragon type trained by Lance himself, a champion.

Me wanting one wasn't just for breeding with Aerodactyl. But the simple fact that, a powerful pokemon from Lance, would be perfect for Pidgeot and my other pokemon to train against to fight a high level opponent on the regular and increase their experience fighting at that level and enhance the speed of their growth.

One must always get the most they can out of any singular situation as far as I'm concerned.

The thing about pokemon that I've noticed over my time here, is that the more intense the training and the more powerful the opponents they face off against, the quicker they grow.

It was why for instance my Golbat was capable of hanging in against the members of my squad who I'd been training for quite a bit longer than him. When I first caught him, he was easily crushed by Raticate, yet by time I sent him here to Viridian City, he was actually capable of putting up a fight against the others like Fearow.

Or take Dragonite for instance. When he battled Aerodactyl, he was overwhelmed completely and only his sheer stubbornness kept him from being taken down easily and kept getting him back up on his feet to fight. Yet, the experience of that battle, then going through a gravity training session and being healed by Blissey, had him nearly on par with Aerodactyl during the free for all sparring battle I had between them after that.

Admittedly, such types of training were even better suited for dragon types, but as a whole all pokemon seemed to be somewhat like saiyans in that regard, the more intense training they went through and the stronger the opponents they fought, the stronger they became in a short amount of time.

This also led into my theory on how Ash's newer pokemon rose in strength so quickly in the original world, putting aside the later generations that were a shit show, Ash and Pikachu had a steady growth through Kanto to Sinnoh, so his newer pokemon even when he started over with just Pikachu, had that powerhouse of a mouse to constantly train with, which allowed them to rise quickly and just why shit like a Treecko that only knew Quick attack and Pound could fight a freaking Hariyama head on when Ash battled Brawly.

"Altaria, huh?" Lance mused and tapped his chin in thought for a moment, before a wide grin spread across his face, "I've got just the one in mind, she should fit the bill."

"Oh?" I blinked, "Is she strong then?" I asked, though that kind of went without saying seeing as it's one of Lance's pokemon. How strong is the question though.

"Very, she's the second strongest of my Altaria, I've got seven of them," Lance flashed me a thumbs up, "I caught her two years back when I was on a trip to Hoenn, she was a little thing back then that got kicked out of her flock for trying to battle for the leadership of it and chafes a bit with me because she isn't as strong as my first Altaria."

Lance looked around for a moment before leaning down to me, "Between you and me, she's not far off of same level as Lorelei's ace Lapras and is quite big for an Altaria," he said lowly as if confiding a great big secret, "I actually bred her with one of my clansmen's Sceptile last year, the Treecko born from it turned out to be quite robust and is already a Sceptile. Give it a year and it might reach a level strong enough to be on my main team."

…Huh.

My eyes widened as I processed that information. If what he said was truth, then this Altaria he was going to give me to breed with Aerodactyl, was definitively on the level of the Elite Four.

Which meant it was a complete powerhouse. No, beyond a mere powerhouse. After all, that battle with Lance was enough to tell me, not even Pidgeot was on that level yet.

Perfect.

But-

"Is that alright?" I asked, raising an eyebrow, "I know I was wanting a strong pokemon, but one like that? And you're fine with just giving her to me?"

"Truth be told, normally such a thing would be completely off the table, Aerodactyl or not," Lance shrugged, "But from what I've seen, you have what it takes to control a pokemon of that level and I know from how you treated your pokemon that you'll take care of her. Besides, while she gets on with most of my pokemon, she doesn't like my main Altaria at all, she chafes under it and feels like she's playing second fiddle to another of her same species."

Well, that was understandable I suppose. It was jarring enough for most people to not be the first pick when it came to things they put a massive amount of effort into, but being the second pick to another near complete same as you beyond even that, must have hit the pride something fierce.

A lot of people thought having a lot of pride in yourself and your abilities was a bad thing, I don't. Pride is important. And I personally would be rightfully pissed off, maybe even resentful of having my pride repeatedly stepped on.

"Alright then, I can work with that," I grinned, "Tell me about her though. What kind of food does she like? What things does she like in general? My Clefable for instance loves learning about stories of the past and legendary pokemon like Rayquaza."

"She's a bit of a glutton actually, she'll eat anything edible you put down in front of her," Lance laughed, "Hobby wise she's pretty simple, beyond battling and eating, she likes singing, though sometimes has a bad habit of putting others to sleep without realising it and-"

Lance was cut off the by doors to the Pokemon Centre opening up and quite the large group making their way into the building. Nurse Joy at the front had an exhausted look on her face and she was followed by Professor Oak and Cerise who were practically vibrating with beaming smiles, trailing right behind them was Delia Ketchum who was chatting away happily to Chloe Cerise and at the back were the presences of an impassive faced Giovanni and Surge who met his eyes, before looking away, an uncomfortable look on his face.

"Can't you ever do anything without causing me more work?" Nurse Joy asked in annoyance.

"My bad?" I offered lamely. It wasn't like I was expecting all this general hubbub when I was making my way here. I just took advantage of it for my own benefit. Like I do a lot of things.

She rolled her eyes and shook her head, "Well whatever, you're here now at least, and hopefully won't cause anymore incidents like this, for a while at least."

"I'll try my best." I laughed.

"No he won't, he had fun," Lance snorted. He looked me in the eye, "I've got your contact details and we've hashed this deal out, so I'll be heading out now, I'll get in contact with you later tonight once I make arrangements on my side."

"Leaving already Lance?" Oak asked, "I was actually hoping to pick your brain about young Jord's Aerodactyl if you had the time."

"Sorry Professor Oak, everyone," Lance cast an apologetic look around at the gathered people, "Unfortunately, freeing up any more time than this is quite troublesome, I only managed this because it was such a grand occasion."

"Well, being the champion of two regions, dealing with your clan duties and being a G-man, it's no wonder," Giovanni spoke up, voice clipped, a faint smirk was spread across his face, "Perhaps its time you cut down on some of those duties, I'm sure if you were to be the champion of only one region your schedule would be opened up quite a bit."

There was something to how he spoke to Lance that I couldn't put a finger on. While his tone was clipped, it was polite.

But I couldn't shake the feeling that there was disdain for Lance in his words.

"I'll be happy to do so when someone strong enough comes along," Lance just smiled pleasantly at the gym leaders words, "Always a pleasure Giovanni, I'll be looking forward to your next attempt to challenge me, I'm sure Lorelei will love another battle with you."

Giovanni's eyes narrowed ever so slightly and if I wasn't paying attention to him as he did, I would have definitely missed it, "Quite." was all he said in response.

Lance's smile widened a bit and he dipped his head to me, "I'll see you later Jord." he said, clapping me on the shoulder, before moving, and with a regal, powerful gait, he made his way towards the building's entrance, his cape billowing gracefully over his shoulders.

A moment later, he disappeared through the doors and all attention returned to me, "Uh…" I trailed off, "What's up?" I greeted them.
